## 3. books: title asc, first page

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.title AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY b.title ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 6. books: publication_year asc

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 9. books: language=en, title

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.title AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.language_code = $1 ORDER BY b.title ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 10. books: publisher (popular), title

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.title AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.publisher = $1 ORDER BY b.title ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 13. books: year range 1900..1950, year sort

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) >= $1::smallint AND b.publication_year IS NOT NULL AND coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) <= $2::smallint ORDER BY coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 14. books: year range + language

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.created_at AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.language_code = $1 AND coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) >= $2::smallint AND b.publication_year IS NOT NULL AND coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) <= $3::smallint ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT 21
```

## 15. books: created_by

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.created_at AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.created_by = $1::bigint ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT 21
```

## 16. books: created_from/created_to (1 day)

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.created_at AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.created_at >= $1 AND b.created_at <= $2 ORDER BY b.created_at ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 19. books: author_id + title sort

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.title AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.id IN (SELECT ab.book_id FROM author_books ab WHERE ab.author_id = $1::bigint) ORDER BY b.title ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 20. books: author name

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.created_at AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.id IN (SELECT ab.book_id FROM author_books ab WHERE ab.author_id IN (SELECT a.id FROM authors a WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND a.search_vector @@ to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1)))) ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT 21
```

## 21. books: title substring (trigram)

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.created_at AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND f_unaccent(b.title) ILIKE '%' || f_unaccent($1) || '%' ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT 21
```


## 23. books: q full-text + language + year

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.version, b.title AS sort_value FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1)) AND b.language_code = $2 AND coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) >= $3::smallint AND b.publication_year IS NOT NULL ORDER BY b.title ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 24. authors: full_name, first page


## 31. authors: name substring (trigram, cyrillic)

```sql
SELECT a.id, a.full_name, a.original_name, a.birth_date, a.death_date, a.country_code, a.biography, a.external_ids, a.created_at, a.created_by, a.updated_at, a.updated_by, a.version, a.full_name AS sort_value FROM authors a WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND f_unaccent(a.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(a.original_name, '')) ILIKE '%' || f_unaccent($1) || '%' ORDER BY a.full_name ASC, a.id ASC LIMIT 21
```

## 32. authors: q full-text


## 38. search all: author name

```sql
SELECT s.type, s.id, s.title, s.subtitle, s.score FROM ((SELECT 'book'::text AS type, c.id, c.title, c.original_title AS subtitle, (ts_rank(c.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1)))::float8  + word_similarity(f_unaccent($1), f_unaccent(c.title))::float8) AS score FROM (SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.search_vector FROM books b       WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND (b.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1))             OR f_unaccent($1) <% f_unaccent(b.title))       ORDER BY b.id DESC LIMIT 1000) c) UNION ALL (SELECT 'author'::text AS type, c.id, c.full_name AS title, c.original_name AS subtitle, (ts_rank(c.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1)))::float8  + word_similarity(f_unaccent($1), f_unaccent(c.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(c.original_name, '')))::float8) AS score FROM (SELECT a.id, a.full_name, a.original_name, a.search_vector FROM authors a       WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND (a.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1))             OR f_unaccent($1) <% f_unaccent(a.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(a.original_name, '')))       ORDER BY a.id DESC LIMIT 1000) c)) s ORDER BY s.score DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT 21
```

## 39. search books: typo 'shadw river'

```sql
SELECT s.type, s.id, s.title, s.subtitle, s.score FROM ((SELECT 'book'::text AS type, c.id, c.title, c.original_title AS subtitle, (ts_rank(c.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1)))::float8  + word_similarity(f_unaccent($1), f_unaccent(c.title))::float8) AS score FROM (SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.search_vector FROM books b       WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND (b.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1))             OR f_unaccent($1) <% f_unaccent(b.title))       ORDER BY b.id DESC LIMIT 1000) c)) s ORDER BY s.score DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT 21
```

## 40. search authors: cyrillic

```sql
SELECT s.type, s.id, s.title, s.subtitle, s.score FROM ((SELECT 'author'::text AS type, c.id, c.full_name AS title, c.original_name AS subtitle, (ts_rank(c.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1)))::float8  + word_similarity(f_unaccent($1), f_unaccent(c.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(c.original_name, '')))::float8) AS score FROM (SELECT a.id, a.full_name, a.original_name, a.search_vector FROM authors a       WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND (a.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($1))             OR f_unaccent($1) <% f_unaccent(a.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(a.original_name, '')))       ORDER BY a.id DESC LIMIT 1000) c)) s ORDER BY s.score DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT 21
```
